CORPORATE PROFILE
四環醫藥控股集團有限公司（「四環醫藥」或「本                Founded in 2001 and listed on the Main Board of The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong
公司」，連同其附屬公司為「本集團」）       （股份代號：       Limited in 2010, Sihuan Pharmaceutical Holdings Group Ltd. (“Sihuan Pharmaceutical”
00460.HK）創立於二零零一年，二零一零年於              or the “Company”, together with its subsidiaries, the “Group”) (Stock Code:
香港聯合交易所有限公司主板上市，是一家以                  00460.HK) is an international medical aesthetics and biopharmaceutical company led
創新為引領，堅持創新驅動，擁有獨立領先的                  and driven by innovation, with a leading independent production, and research and
自主生產、研究與開發（「研發」）技術平台，具                development (“R&D”) technology platform, a rich global product pipeline and a mature
備豐富的全球化產品管線和成熟卓越銷售體系                  and excellent sales system. Focusing on high-growth therapeutic areas such as medical
的國際化醫美及生物製藥企業。四環醫藥聚焦                  aesthetics, oncology, metabolism, diabetes, cardiovascular and cerebrovascular, it
醫 美、腫 瘤、代 謝、糖 尿 病、心 腦 血 管 等 高 增       adheres to its overall strategic objective of “Adhering to the full-speed promotion of a
長 治 療 領 域，一 直 秉 承「堅 持 全 速 推 進 四 環 醫   two-wheeled strategy of Sihuan medical aesthetics and biopharmaceuticals” to build a
美及生物製藥雙輪驅動戰略」的整體戰略目標                  leading medical aesthetics and biopharmaceutical enterprise in China.
來打造中國領先的醫美及生物製藥企業。

管理層討論及分析
MANAGEMENT DISCUSSION AND ANALYSIS


二零二五年四環醫藥順利實現全年扭虧為盈，                       In 2025, Sihuan Pharmaceutical successfully achieved a full-year turnaround from
完成經營週期關鍵歷史性拐點，正式邁入戰略                       loss to profit, marking a historic inflection point in its operating cycle and formally
落 地 與 業 績 釋 放 並 行 的 價 值 兌 現 週 期。二 零        entering a value-realisation phase in which strategy implementation and performance
二六年上半年，本集團持續錨定「醫美 + 創新藥」                   release proceeded in parallel. In the first half of 2026, the Group continued to anchor
雙 輪 驅 動 中 長 期 核 心 發 展 戰 略，構 建「醫 美 作        its medium- to long-term core development strategy of dual-engine drive through
為第一增長支柱、創新藥打造第二增長曲線、                       “medical aesthetics + innovative drugs”, and built a multi-tiered business layout
仿製藥穩定供給經營性現金流並逐步剝離或處                       under which “medical aesthetics served as the primary growth pillar, innovative drugs
置仿製藥業務和非核心資產」的立體化業務佈                       forged a second growth curve, generic drugs provided stable operating cash flow,
局。報告期內，本集團醫美業務完成由單品銷                       and engaged in gradual divestment or disposal of generic drugs and non-core assets”.
售向醫美整體解決方案服務商的戰略轉型，依                       During the reporting period, the Group’s medical aesthetics business completed its
託核心產品搭建全渠道協同運營體系；創新藥                       strategic transformation from single-product sales to a provider of integrated medical
兩大平台商業化進程持續提速，板塊虧損持續                       aesthetics solutions, and established an omni-channel coordinated operating system
收窄，盈利改善路徑清晰；仿製藥板塊穩步推                       anchored by its core products. Commercialisation of the two major innovative drug
進資產結構優化與非核心業務剝離，持續發揮                       platforms continued to accelerate, segment losses continued to narrow, and the path to
現金流壓艙石作用。與此同時，本集團前瞻佈                       profitability improvement remained clear. The generic drugs segment steadily advanced
局 AI 技術賦能全產業鏈研發，海內外商業化網                    asset structure optimisation and the divestment of non-core businesses, and continued
絡同步拓展，多層次資本運作有序落地，在醫                       to play its role as a cash-flow ballast. Meanwhile, the Group proactively deployed AI
美管線迭代、創新藥臨床註冊、跨國產業合作、                      technology to empower R&D across the entire industrial chain, expanded domestic and
平台資本化、渠道體系建設、前沿技術平台搭                       overseas commercialisation networks in parallel, and orderly implemented multi-tier
建等領域取得多項里程碑進展，持續夯實本集                       capital market initiatives. Multiple milestone advances were achieved in areas including
團中長期高質量可持續發展根基，為全年經營                       medical aesthetics pipeline iteration, innovative drug clinical registration, cross-border
目標落地、長期股東價值提升築牢基礎。                         industrial cooperation, platform capitalisation, channel system building and frontier
                                           technology platform construction, continuously consolidating the foundation for the
                                           Group’s medium- to long-term high-quality and sustainable development, and laying a
                                           solid base for the delivery of full-year operating targets and the enhancement of long-
                                           term shareholder value.

 的全品類合規產品矩陣築牢競爭壁壘；                  barriers by leveraging an industry-scarce, full-category compliant product matrix.

「星 火 計 劃」滲 透 下 沉 市 場，構 建 頭 部 連      Plan”, penetration into lower-tier markets is deepened to build a tiered channel
 鎖、區域單體、下沉門店分層渠道生態，                 ecosystem comprising leading chains, regional stand-alone institutions and lower-

及 TOP500 頭部機構全覆蓋，標準化聯合              coverage and full coverage of the TOP500 leading institutions, while standardised
套 餐 有 效 帶 動 再 生 產 品 銷 售。產 品 端，      combination packages effectively drive sales of regenerative products. On the

    展。軒 竹 生 物 科 技 股 份 有 限 公 司（「軒 竹             Co., Ltd. (“Xuanzhu Biopharm”) (Stock Code: 02575.HK) focuses on the R&D of
    生物」） （股份代號：02575.HK）聚焦腫瘤、                  innovative drugs in oncology, digestion and metabolism. Commercialisation of its
    消 化 和 代 謝 領 域 創 新 藥 的 研 發，三 款 核            three core products – Xuan Yue Ning, Xuan Fei Ning and An Jiu Wei – continued
    心 產 品 軒 悅 寧 ®、軒 菲 寧 ®、安 久 衛 ® 商            to accelerate: Xuan Yue Ning became the only CDK inhibitor in China covering
    業化提速：軒悅寧 ® 成為國內唯一覆蓋激                       the full treatment course of advanced breast cancer with hormone receptor
    素 受 體 (HR) 陽 性、人 表 皮 生 長 因 子 受 體           positive and human epidermal growth factor receptor 2 negative (“HR+/HER2-”);
    2(HER2) 陰性（「HR+/HER2-」）晚期乳腺癌               pivotal Phase III clinical study results were published in a leading international
    全 療 程 的 CDK 抑 制 劑，重 磅 臨 床 III 期 研          journal, fully validating its efficacy; Xuan Fei Ning was included in multiple

仿製藥業務是本集團穩定現金流基石，                 The generic drugs business is the cornerstone of the Group’s stable cash flow
持 續 為 醫 美、創 新 藥 兩 大 核 心 高 成 長 板   and continuously channels resources to the two core high-growth segments of
塊輸血。受集採、重點監控目錄政策影響，               medical aesthetics and innovative drugs. Affected by volume-based procurement

比 下 滑。公 司 戰 略 由 規 模 擴 張 轉 向 價 值   and the segment’s revenue and profit for the current period declined year on year.
優 化，主 動 剝 離 仿 製 藥 及 非 核 心 低 效 資   The Company’s strategy has shifted from scale expansion to value optimisation,
產，所 釋 放 的 資 金 與 管 理 資 源，將 全 部     with proactive divestment of generic drugs business and non-core, low-efficiency
聚 焦 核 心 賽 道，資 產 處 置 同 步 增 厚 當 期   assets. Capital and management resources released will be fully focused on core
收 益。同 時 持 續 補 充 優 質 仿 製 藥 品 種，    tracks, while asset disposals concurrently thickened current-period earnings.

結語                                   CONCLUSION
下一階段，本集團將持續以 AI 數字化技術賦能              In the next stage, the Group will continue to take AI digital technology empowerment
研發全產業鏈作為核心創新驅動力，以國內深                 of R&D across the full industrial chain as the core innovation driver; broaden long-term
耕頭部渠道、全球多區域雙向佈局拓寬長期增                 growth boundaries through deep cultivation of leading domestic channels and two-way

（三）仿製藥及非核心業務分部進展：                    (III) Progress of the generic drugs and non-core business
  「現金牛」支撐「醫美 + 創新藥」核                       segment: “cash cow” supporting development of the

   進                                       structural optimisation advancing steadily
   報 告 期 內，仿 製 藥 業 務 作 為 本 集 團 穩 定       During the reporting period, the generic drugs business, as a stable cash-flow
   的 現 金 流 來 源，持 續 為 高 成 長 的 醫 美 與       source of the Group, continuously provided funding support for the high-growth
   高價值的創新藥核心業務提供資金支撐。                    medical aesthetics and high-value innovative drugs core businesses. The Group
   本 集 團 在 售 仿 製 藥 產 品 近 百 款，覆 蓋 心       has nearly one hundred generic drug products on sale, covering key therapeutic
   腦 血 管、抗 感 染、神 經 系 統 等 關 鍵 治 療         areas including cardiovascular and cerebrovascular, anti-infective and nervous
   領 域。但 受 國 家 藥 品 集 中 帶 量 採 購、重         system diseases. However, affected by the continuous deepening of policies such
   點 監 控 目 錄 等 政 策 持 續 深 化 影 響，仿 製       as national volume-based drug procurement and key monitoring catalogues, the
   藥行業整體價格體系與利潤空間承壓，                     overall pricing system and profit space of the generic drugs industry have come
   本集團部分存量產品的收入及盈利受到                     under pressure, and revenue and profitability of certain of the Group’s existing
   階 段 性 影 響。報 告 期 內，本 集 團 仿 製 藥         products have been subject to phased impact. During the reporting period, the
   分部實現收入約人民幣 206.5 百萬元，同                Group’s generic medicine segment recorded revenue of approximately RMB206.5
   比 下 降 58.9%；實 現 分 部 業 績 稅 前 虧 損       million, representing a year-on-year decrease of 58.9%; and recorded a segment
   74.1 百 萬 元，同 比 下 降 144.0%。面 對 仿       results of a loss before tax of RMB74.1 million, representing a year-on-year
   製藥業務持續受國家集採等政策壓力影                     decrease of 144.0%. Facing continued pressure on the generic drugs business
   響，本 集 團 持 續 推 進 仿 製 藥 及 非 核 心 業       from policies such as national volume-based procurement, the Group continued
   務 優 化 及 資 產 整 合，相 關 資 產 剝 離 於 該       to advance optimisation of the generic drugs and non-core business and asset
   期間產生收益約人民幣 154.2 百萬元，進                integration; related asset divestments generated gains of approximately RMB154.2

人力資源及僱員薪金                                  Human resources and remuneration of employees
人力資源是本集團在充滿挑戰的環境中得以成                       Human resources are indispensable assets to the Group’s success in a competitive
功的不可或缺資產。本集團致力為全體僱員提                       environment. The Group is committed to providing competitive remuneration packages
供具競爭力的薪酬待遇，定期檢討人力資源政                       to all the employees and regularly reviewing human resources policies, to encourage
策，以鼓勵僱員努力提升本公司價值及促進本                       employees to work towards enhancing the value of the Company and promoting the
公司的可持續增長。本集團亦已採納購股權計                       sustainable growth of the Company. The Group has also adopted share option scheme
劃及股份獎勵計劃，以表揚及獎勵員工對本集                       and share award scheme to recognise and reward the contribution of the employees for
團之營運及未來發展作出的貢獻。本集團持續                       the benefit of the Group’s operations and future development. The Group continues
推動人才培養與發展體系建設，圍繞不同層級                       to promote the building of talent training and development system, and conducts
崗位任職能力標準展開線上與線下的培訓工作，                      online and offline training based on the competency standards for positions at different
促進本集團人才的培育與發展，保障各類人才                       levels to promote the cultivation and development of talents in the Group and ensure
的持續供給。                                     continuous supply of various types of talents.

引及挽留所作貢獻有利、將有利或可能有利於                        otherwise maintaining on-going relationship with the grantees whose contributions are,
本集團長期發展的承授人，或以其他方式與彼                        will be or are likely to be beneficial to the long-term growth of the Group.
等維持持續關係。

二零二六年股權激勵計劃之目的                           Purpose of the 2026 Share Incentive Scheme
二零二六年股權激勵計劃之目的為：(a) 為本公                  The purpose of the 2026 Share Incentive Scheme is: (a) to provide the Company with
司提供靈活方式，以酬謝、激勵、留聘、獎勵、                    a flexible means of remunerating, incentivizing, retaining, rewarding, compensating
補償及╱或向合資格參與者（定義見下文）提供                    and/or providing benefits to Eligible Participants (as hereinafter defined); (b) to align
福利；(b) 透過向該等合資格參與者提供獲取本                  the interests of Eligible Participants with those of the Company and Shareholders by
公司股權之機會，使合資格參與者之利益與本                     providing such Eligible Participants with the opportunity to acquire shareholding interests
公司及股東之利益保持一致；及 (c) 鼓勵合資格                 in the Company; and (c) to encourage Eligible Participants to contribute to the long-
參與者為本公司之長期增長及盈利能力作出貢                     term growth and profitability of the Company and to enhance the value of the Company
獻，並提升本公司及其股份之價值，以惠及本                     and its Shares for the benefit of the Company and Shareholders as a whole. In the
公司及股東之整體利益。於二零二六年股權激                     2026 Share Incentive Scheme, references to new Shares include Treasury Shares, and
勵計劃中，對新股份之提述包括庫存股份，對                     references to the issue of Shares include the transfer of Treasury Shares. The Company
發行股份之提述包括轉讓庫存股份。本公司可                     may use Treasury Shares to satisfy the exercise or vesting of Awards (as hereinafter
使用庫存股份以滿足二零二六年股權激勵計劃                     defined) under the 2026 Share Incentive Scheme.

1.   公 司 及 集 團資料                                1.   CORPORATE AND GROUP INFORMATION
     四環醫藥控股集團有限公司（「本公司」）                             Sihuan Pharmaceutical Holdings Group Ltd. (the “Company”) is incorporated in
     根據百慕達公司法於百慕達註冊成立為                               Bermuda under the Bermuda Companies Act as an exempted company.
     獲豁免公司。


     本 公 司 為 投 資 控 股 公 司。本 公 司 及 其 附                 The Company is an investment holding company. The principal activities of the
     屬公司（統稱「本集團」）的主要業務為於                             Company and its subsidiaries (together, the “Group”) are the research and
     中華人民共和國（     「中國」）研究及開發（「研                      development (“R&D”), manufacture and sale of pharmaceutical and medical
     發」）、製造及銷售醫藥及醫美產品。                               aesthetic products in the People’s Republic of China (the “PRC”).


     本 公 司 註 冊 辦 事 處 地 址 為 Richmond                  The address of the Company’s registered office is Richmond House, 12 Par-la-Ville
     House, 12 Par-la-Ville Road, Hamilton HM        Road, Hamilton HM 08, Bermuda. The address of the principal place of business
     08, Bermuda。本集團香港主要營業地點                         of the Group in Hong Kong is Room 4905, Office Tower, Convention Plaza, 1
     為香港灣仔港灣道1號會展廣場辦公大                               Harbour Road, Wanchai, Hong Kong, and the address of the principal place of
     樓 4905 室，及北京主要營業地點為中國                           business in Beijing is 22/F, Building 4, Zhubang 2000, West Balizhuang, Chaoyang

     號樓 22 層（郵編：100025）。


2.   編 製 基 準 及會計政策變動                            2.   BASIS OF PREPARATION AND CHANGES IN
                                                     ACCOUNTING POLICIES
     2.1 編製基準                                        2.1 Basis of preparation
          截至二零二六年六月三十日止六                                   The interim condensed consolidated financial information for the six
          個月的中期簡明綜合財務資料乃                                   months ended 30 June 2026 has been prepared in accordance with IAS 34
          根 據 國 際 會 計 準 則 第 34 號中 期 財                      Interim Financial Reporting . The interim condensed consolidated financial
          務報告 編製。中期簡明綜合財務資                                 information does not include all the information and disclosures required
          料不包括年度財務報表中規定的                                   in the annual financial statements, and should be read in conjunction with
          所有資料及披露且應與本集團截                                   the Group’s annual consolidated financial statements for the year ended 31
          至二零二五年十二月三十一日止                                   December 2025.
          年度的年度綜合財務報表一併閱讀。


     2.2 會計政策變動及披露事項                                 2.2 Changes in accounting policies and disclosures
           編製中期簡明綜合財務資料所採                                  The accounting policies adopted in the preparation of the interim
           納的會計政策與編製本集團截至                                  condensed consolidated financial information are consistent with those
           二零二五年十二月三十一日止年                                  applied in the preparation of the Group’s annual consolidated financial

3.   分部資料                                 3.   SEGMENT INFORMATION
     就 管 理 而 言，本 集 團 基 於 其 產 品 及 服 務           For management purposes, the Group is organised into business units based
     劃分業務單位，三個可報告經營分部如下：                       on their products and services and has three reportable operating segments as
                                               follows:


     (a)   醫美產品分部從事提供光電設備、                     (a)   the medical aesthetic products segment engaging in the provision of
           體雕、皮膚管理及其他服務以提供                           optoelectronic devices, body sculpturing, skin care and other services to
           無創醫美綜合解決方案；                               provide comprehensive non-invasive medical aesthetics solutions;


     (b)   創新藥及其他藥品分部；及                        (b)   the innovative medicine and other medicine segment; and


     (c)   仿製藥分部。                              (c)   the generic medicine segment.


      管理層獨立監察本集團經營分部的業績，                       Management monitors the results of the Group’s operating segments separately
      以作出有關資源分配及表現評估的決策。                       for the purpose of making decisions about resource allocation and performance
      分部表現乃根據可報告分部溢利╱虧損                        assessment. Segment performance is evaluated based on reportable segment
     （其為經調整除稅前溢利╱虧損的計量）                        profit/loss, which is measured in a form of an adjusted profit/loss before tax. The
      予 以 評 估。經 調 整 除 稅 前 溢 利 ╱ 虧 損 的          adjusted profit/loss before tax is measured consistently with the Group’s profit/loss
      計量與本集團之除稅前溢利╱虧損的計                        before tax except that interest income, non-lease-related finance costs, dividend
      量 一 致，惟 利 息 收 入、非 租 賃 相 關 融 資            income, fair value gains/losses from the Group’s financial instruments as well as
      成 本、股 息 收 入、本 集 團 金 融 工 具 的 公            head office and corporate expenses are excluded from such measurement.
      平值收益╱虧損連同總部及公司開支不
      計入該計量內。

            益的金融資產，因為其合約現金流量並                                their contractual cash flows are not solely payments of principal and interest.
            非僅是本金及利息付款。

17. 以 股 份 為 基礎的付款                           17. SHARE-BASED PAYMENTS
      (a)      本公司購股權計劃                          (a)   Share Option Scheme of the Company
               本 公 司 實 施 購 股 權 計 劃（「購 股 權              The Company operates a share option scheme (the “Share Option
               計 劃」），旨 在 向 為 本 集 團 成 功 經               Scheme”) for the purpose of providing incentives and rewards to eligible
               營作出貢獻的合資格參與者提供                          participants who contribute to the success of the Group’s operations.
               激勵及獎勵。購股權計劃的合資格                         Eligible participants of the Share Option Scheme include the Company’s
               參與者包括本公司董事（包括獨立                         directors, including independent non-executive directors, other employees
               非執行董事）、本集團其他僱員、                         of the Group, suppliers of goods or services to the Group, customers of the

21. 金 融 工 具 的公平值及公平                          21. FAIR VALUE AND FAIR VALUE HIERARCHY OF
    值 層 級（續）                                     FINANCIAL INSTRUMENTS (continued)
     按公平值計入損益的非上市股權投資的                              The fair values of unlisted equity investments designated at fair value through
     公 平 值，乃 根 據 並 非 由 可 見 市 價 或 比 率                profit or loss have been estimated using a market-based valuation technique
     支 持 之 假 設，使 用 市 場 估 值 方 法 預 測。                 based on assumptions that are not supported by observable market prices or rates.
     估 值 要 求 董 事 根 據 行 業、規 模、槓 桿 及                  The valuation requires the directors to determine comparable public companies
     戰 略 釐 定 可 資 比 較 公 眾 公 司，並 就 識 別                based on industry, size, leverage and strategy, and to calculate an appropriate
     出的各可資比較公司計算合適價格倍數，                             price multiple, such as enterprise value to earnings before interest, taxes,
     例 如 企 業 價 值 對 除 利 息、稅 項、折 舊 及                  depreciation and amortisation (“EV/EBITDA”) multiple and price to earnings (“P/E”)
     攤銷前盈利（「EV/EBITDA」）倍數及價格對                       multiple, for each comparable company identified. The multiple is calculated by
     盈利（「P/E」）倍數。倍數乃以可資比較公                          dividing the enterprise value of the comparable company by an earnings measure.
     司的企業價值除以盈利衡量計算得出。                              The trading multiple is then discounted for considerations such as illiquidity and
     交易倍數以可資比較公司根據公司獨有                              size differences between the comparable companies based on company-specific
     因 素 及 情 況 的 考 慮 因 素，例 如 非 流 通 性                facts and circumstances. The discounted multiple is applied to the corresponding
     及 規 模 差 異，予 以 貼 現。貼 現 倍 數 用 於                  earnings measure of the unlisted equity investments to measure the fair value.
     非上市股權投資的相應盈利衡量以計量                              The directors believe that the estimated fair values resulting from the valuation
     公 平 值。董 事 認 為 估 值 方 法 得 出 的 估 計                technique, which are recorded in the consolidated statement of financial position,
     公平值（記錄於綜合財務狀況表）及相關                             and the related changes in fair values, which are recorded in the consolidated
     公平值變動（記錄於綜合損益及其他全面                             statement of profit or loss and other comprehensive income, are reasonable, and
     收益表）屬合理，並為報告期末最合適的                             that they were the most appropriate values at the end of the reporting period.
     價值。

     本 集 團 投 資 於 非 上 市 投 資，即 中 國 內 地                The Group invests in unlisted investments, which represent wealth management
     銀 行 發 行 的 理 財 產 品。本 集 團 已 使 用 貼                products issued by banks in Chinese mainland. The Group has estimated the fair
     現 現 金 流 量 估 值 模 型，根 據 類 似 年 期 及                value of these unlisted investments by using a discounted cash flow valuation
     風險的工具的市場利率估算該等非上市                              model based on the market interest rates of instruments with similar terms and
     投資的公平值。                                        risks.

     對於按公平值計入其他全面收益的非上                              For the fair value of the unlisted equity investments at fair value through other
     市 股 權 投 資 的 公 平 值，管 理 層 已 估 計 使                comprehensive income, management has estimated the potential effect of using
     用合理可行的替代方法作為估值模型參                              reasonably possible alternatives as inputs to the valuation model.
     數的潛在影響。
